::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 24, s. 9
- 1999, c. 31, s. 103(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report in Public Accounts

**[[24.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24.2 .sectionLabel}** Forgiveness of a debt
or obligation under this or any other Act of Parliament during a fiscal
year shall be reported in the Public Accounts for that year in such form
as the Treasury Board may direct.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 24, s. 9
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debt write-off regulations

- **[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the
  Treasury Board may make regulations respecting the writing off, in
  whole or in part, of any debt or obligation due to Her Majesty or any
  claim by Her Majesty, and without restricting the generality of the
  foregoing, may make regulations prescribing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the criteria for determining whether any debt,
    obligation or claim may be written off;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the requirements to be met and the procedures to be
    followed before any debt, obligation or claim may be written off;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the information and records to be kept in respect
    of debts, obligations and claims that are written off.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No debt, obligation or claim that is included in the
  statement of assets and liabilities of Canada referred to in
  subparagraph 64(2)(a)(iii), the writing off of which would result in a
  charge to an appropriation, shall be written off unless the amount to
  be written off is included as a budgetary expenditure in an
  appropriation Act or any other Act of Parliament.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of write-off

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The writing off of any debt, obligation or claim
  pursuant to this section does not affect any right of Her Majesty to
  collect or recover the debt, obligation or claim.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report in Public Accounts

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Any debt, obligation or claim written off under this
  or any other Act of Parliament during a fiscal year shall be reported
  in the Public Accounts for that year in such form as the Treasury
  Board may direct.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1991, c. 24, s. 10\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 25
- 1991, c. 24, ss. 10, 50(F)
- 1999, c. 31, s. 104(F)
:::

## [PART III]{.HLabel1}[Public Disbursements]{.HTitleText1} {#h-228550 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payments out of C.R.F.

**[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the
[Constitution Acts, 1867 to 1982](/eng/Const/), no payments shall be
made out of the Consolidated Revenue Fund without the authority of
Parliament.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. F-10, s. 19
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Estimates

**[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** All estimates of
expenditures submitted to Parliament shall be in respect of payments
during the fiscal year to which the estimates relate and expenditures
that will be incurred during that fiscal year.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 27
- 1991, c. 24, s. 11
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Warrant of Governor General

**[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** Where an appropriation is
made for any purpose in any Act of Parliament for granting to Her
Majesty any sum of money to defray expenses of the federal public
administration for a fiscal year, no payment shall be made pursuant to
that appropriation out of the Consolidated Revenue Fund unless a
warrant, prepared on the order of the Governor in Council, has been
signed by the Governor General authorizing expenditures to be charged
against the appropriation, and no payments in excess of the amount of
expenditures so authorized shall be made.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 28
- 1999, c. 31, s. 105(F)
- 2003, c. 22, s. 224(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-28-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 28"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment of guarantee

- **[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  a guarantee has been given under the authority of Parliament by or on
  behalf of Her Majesty for the payment of any debt or obligation, any
  amount required to be paid by the terms of the guarantee may, subject
  to the Act authorizing the guarantee, be paid out of the Consolidated
  Revenue Fund.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authority for guarantee

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An authority referred to in subsection (1) may be
  contained in an appropriation Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. F-10, s. 22
- 1980-81-82-83, c. 170, s. 6
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expenditure of revenues by departmental
corporations

- **[[29.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  departmental corporation may expend during a fiscal year, for the
  purposes of the departmental corporation, any revenues that it
  receives in that fiscal year through the conduct of its operations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expenditure by departments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A department may, in respect of its approved programs
  or authorized expenditures, be authorized by an appropriation Act

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes that are specified in that Act, to
    expend revenues that it receives in a fiscal year through the
    conduct of its operations to offset expenditures that it incurs in
    that fiscal year; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for such purposes and with such drawdown limit as
    are specified in that Act, to establish a revolving fund.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment of revolving fund

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The purposes and drawdown limit of a revolving fund
  referred to in subsection (2) may be amended by means of an
  appropriation Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The operation of a revolving fund and the spending of
  revenues pursuant to this or any other Act of Parliament is, in
  addition to any limitation imposed by statute, subject to such terms
  and conditions as the Treasury Board may direct.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 24, s. 12
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Internal support services

- **[[29.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  department may provide internal support services to and receive
  internal support services from one or more other departments, and the
  provision of those services may be through collaboration among
  departments.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Written agreement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any department providing internal support services to
  another department must enter into an agreement in writing with that
  department respecting those services.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not authorize a department to
  provide internal support services if, under an Act of Parliament,
  order of the Governor in Council or direction of Treasury Board,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} those services may only be provided by another
    department or body;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} departments must obtain those services from another
    department or body; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it is precluded from doing so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[internal support
  services]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[internal support
  services]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means administrative activities that
  support

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} human resources management services;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} financial management services;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} information management services;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} information technology services;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} communications services;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} real property services;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} materiel services;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} acquisition services; or

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any other administrative service that is designated
    by order of the Governor in Council.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2011, c. 15, s. 34
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payments urgently required

- **[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (1.1), where a
  payment is urgently required for the public good

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at any time that Parliament is not in session from
    the date of a dissolution until 60 days following the date fixed for
    the return of the writs at the general election immediately
    following that dissolution, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} there is no other appropriation pursuant to which
    the payment may be made,

  the Governor in Council, on the report of the President of the
  Treasury Board that there is no appropriation for the payment and the
  report of the appropriate Minister that the payment is urgently
  required for the public good, may, by order, direct the preparation of
  a special warrant to be signed by the Governor General authorizing the
  payment to be made out of the Consolidated Revenue Fund.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No special warrants when Parliament
  prorogued

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council shall not, in the 60 days
  referred to in subsection (1), direct the preparation of a special
  warrant referred to in that subsection when Parliament is not in
  session on any of those days by virtue of the fact that it is
  prorogued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special warrant

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A special warrant issued pursuant to this section
  shall for the purposes of this Act be deemed to be an appropriation
  for the fiscal year in which the warrant is issued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication and report

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every warrant issued under this section shall be
  published in the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) within 30
  days after it is issued, and a statement showing all warrants issued
  under this section and the amounts of those warrants shall be laid by
  the President of the Treasury Board before the House of Commons within
  15 days after the commencement of the next ensuing session of
  Parliament.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsequent appropriation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a special warrant has been issued pursuant to
  this section, the amounts appropriated thereby shall be deemed to be
  included in and not to be in addition to the amounts appropriated by
  the Act of Parliament enacted next thereafter for granting to Her
  Majesty sums of money to defray expenses of the federal public
  administration for a fiscal year.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1997, c. 5, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 30
- 1997, c. 5, s. 1
- 2003, c. 22, s. 224(E)
- [2020, c. 4, s. 1](#1238684-1238940){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1238684-1238940.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-30-20200313.html "Link to previous version of section 30"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appropriation allotments

- **[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} At the
  commencement of each fiscal year or at such other times as the
  Treasury Board may direct, the deputy head or other person charged
  with the administration of a service for which there is an
  appropriation by Parliament or an item included in estimates then
  before the House of Commons shall, unless otherwise directed by the
  Board, prepare a division of the appropriation or item into allotments
  in the form detailed in the estimates submitted to Parliament for the
  appropriation or item or in such other form as the Board may prescribe
  and shall submit the division to the Board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Allotments not to be varied without
  approval

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a division required to be submitted to the
  Treasury Board pursuant to subsection (1) is approved by the Board,
  the allotments shall not be varied or amended without the approval of
  the Board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Departmental control of allotments

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The deputy head or other person charged with the
  administration of a service for which a division is required to be
  prepared pursuant to subsection (1) shall ensure by an adequate system
  of internal control and audit that the allotments provided in that
  division are not exceeded.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 31
- 1991, c. 24, s. 50(F)
- 1999, c. 31, s. 106(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Credits transferred

**[[31.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31.1 .sectionLabel}** If an order is made
under section 2 of the [Public Service Rearrangement and Transfer of
Duties Act](/eng/acts/P-34), all of the unexpended money authorized by
an Act of Parliament to be paid and applied for the purposes of any
power, duty or function or control or supervision of a portion of the
federal public administration that is transferred as a result of the
operation of sections 2 and 3 of that Act is deemed to have been
appropriated for the purposes of the power, duty, function, control or
supervision to the department in, or portion of, the federal public
administration to which it has been transferred.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2011, c. 15, s. 35
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Control of commitments

- **[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  contract or other arrangement providing for a payment shall be entered
  into unless, to discharge any debt that will be due under the contract
  or other arrangement during the fiscal year in which the contract or
  other arrangement is entered into, there is a sufficient unencumbered
  balance available out of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an appropriation by Parliament to which the payment
    will be charged;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an item included in estimates then before the House
    of Commons to which the payment will relate;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a commitment limit in an appropriation Act to which
    the payment will relate; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} revenues received or estimated revenues set out in
    estimates, in the case of a payment that will be charged to an
    authority --- under an appropriation Act or any other Act of
    Parliament --- to expend revenues.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record of commitments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The deputy head or other person charged with the
  administration of a program for which there is an appropriation by
  Parliament or an item included in estimates then before the House of
  Commons shall, as the Treasury Board may prescribe, establish
  procedures and maintain records respecting the control of financial
  commitments chargeable to each appropriation or item.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 32
- 1999, c. 31, s. 107(F)
- 2017, c. 33, s. 261
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-32-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 32"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requisitions

- **[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  charge shall be made against an appropriation except on the
  requisition of the appropriate Minister of the department for which
  the appropriation was made or of a person authorized in writing by
  that Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every requisition for a payment out of the
  Consolidated Revenue Fund shall be in such form, accompanied by such
  documents and certified in such manner as the Treasury Board may
  prescribe by regulation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When requisition not to be made

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No requisition shall be made pursuant to
  subsection (1) for a payment that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} would not be a lawful charge against the
    appropriation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} would result in an expenditure in excess of the
    appropriation; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} would reduce the balance available in the
    appropriation so that it would not be sufficient to meet the
    commitments charged against it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reference to Treasury Board

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The appropriate Minister may transmit to the Treasury
  Board any requisition with respect to which that Minister desires the
  direction of the Board, and the Board may order that payment be made
  or refused.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. F-10, s. 26
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment for work, goods or services

- **[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  payment shall be made in respect of any part of the federal public
  administration unless, in addition to any other voucher or certificate
  that is required, the deputy of the appropriate Minister, or another
  person authorized by that Minister, certifies

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a payment for the performance of
    work, the supply of goods or the rendering of services,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that the work has been performed, the goods
      supplied or the service rendered, as the case may be, and that the
      price charged is according to the contract, or if not specified by
      the contract, is reasonable,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} where, pursuant to the contract, a payment is to
      be made before the completion of the work, delivery of the goods
      or rendering of the service, as the case may be, that the payment
      is according to the contract, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} where, in accordance with the policies and
      procedures prescribed under subsection (2), payment is to be made
      in advance of verification, that the claim for payment is
      reasonable; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any other payment, that the payee is
    eligible for or entitled to the payment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Policies and procedures

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Treasury Board may prescribe policies and
  procedures to be followed to give effect to the certification and
  verification required under subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 34
- 1991, c. 24, s. 13
- 2003, c. 22, s. 224(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-34-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 34"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[instruction for
payment]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section and section 36, [[instruction for
  payment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an instrument or other instruction
  for the payment of money, but does not include a requisition under
  section 33.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of payments out of C.R.F.

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every payment out of the Consolidated Revenue Fund
  shall be made under the direction and control of the Receiver General
  by the issuance of an instruction for payment, in such form and
  authenticated in such manner as the Treasury Board may direct.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Claim for settlement

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An amount set out in an instruction for payment
  issued under subsection (2), less any amount charged back as a result
  of a reconciliation pursuant to section 36, may be paid out of the
  Consolidated Revenue Fund where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a claim for settlement of the amount is made by a
    member of the Canadian Payments Association or by a person
    authorized by the Receiver General to make a claim for settlement;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the claim is made in the prescribed manner and is
    accompanied by the prescribed evidence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescription of manner of claim

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Receiver General may prescribe the manner of
  making a claim for settlement and the evidence that must accompany the
  claim.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 35
- 1991, c. 24, s. 14
- 1999, c. 31, s. 108(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reconciliation of claim with evidence and
instruction for payment

- **[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  a payment out of the Consolidated Revenue Fund is made in respect of a
  claim for settlement, the Receiver General shall examine the claim and
  make a reconciliation between the claim and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the supporting evidence; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the instruction for payment to which the claim
    relates.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Destruction of instructions for
  payment, records, etc.

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Treasury Board may, on the recommendation of the
  Receiver General and with the approval of the Auditor General of
  Canada, make regulations governing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the destruction of records of instructions for
    payment, including payment instruments, after the amounts specified
    in the instructions for payment have been paid;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the destruction of claims for settlement; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the destruction of records of instructions for
    settlement, including instruments for settlement within or between
    departments, after settlement has been effected.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 36
- 1991, c. 24, s. 14
- 1999, c. 31, s. 109(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Lapse

**[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37 .sectionLabel}** The balance of an
appropriation that remains unexpended at the end of a fiscal year or
such longer period as may be specified in an appropriation Act or any
other Act of Parliament, after adjustment for the recording of debts
incurred and other amounts due or owing referred to in section 37.1,
shall lapse.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 37
- 1991, c. 24, s. 15
- 1996, c. 18, s. 7
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unpaid debts

- **[[37.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to such directions as the
  Treasury Board may make, a debt incurred by Her Majesty for work
  performed, goods received or services rendered before the end of a
  fiscal year, and any amount due or owing under a contract,
  contribution or other similar arrangement entered into before the end
  of the fiscal year that remains unpaid at the end of the fiscal year,
  shall be recorded as a charge against the appropriation to which it
  relates.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), in such period as the
  Treasury Board may specify or, if no such period is specified, at any
  time, a payment may be made for the purpose of settling any debt or
  other amount due or owing that has been recorded as a charge against
  an appropriation pursuant to subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Settlement in excess of appropriation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The discharge or settlement of a debt or other amount
  due or owing that has been recorded as a charge against an
  appropriation pursuant to subsection (1), or any part thereof, and
  that was in excess of the balance then remaining in the appropriation

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} constitutes a first charge against the next
    appropriation in the year of discharge or settlement; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} operates to reduce the available spending authority
    of that next appropriation by the lesser of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the discharge or settlement, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the excess.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment in excess of appropriation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where, despite paragraph 33(3)(b), a payment is made
  that results in an expenditure that is in excess of an appropriation,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount by which the expenditure exceeds the
    balance then remaining in the appropriation constitutes a first
    charge against the next appropriation of the immediately subsequent
    fiscal year; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the available spending authority of that next
    appropriation is reduced by that amount.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 24, s. 15
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Accountable advances

- **[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Treasury Board may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} authorizing the making of accountable advances
    chargeable to the appropriation for the service in respect of which
    the advance is made; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} providing for the repayment of, accounting for and
    recovery of accountable advances.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recovery

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any accountable advance or any portion thereof that
  is not repaid, accounted for or recovered in accordance with the
  regulations may be recovered out of any moneys payable by Her Majesty
  to the person to whom the advance was made or, where the person is
  deceased, out of any moneys payable by Her Majesty to the estate of
  that person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every accountable advance that is not repaid,
  accounted for or recovered by the end of the fiscal year in which it
  was made shall be reported in the Public Accounts for that year.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. F-10, s. 31
- 1980-81-82-83, c. 170, s. 9
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Crediting of refunds

**[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** Subject to such
directions as the Treasury Board may make, any amount received as

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a refund of an expenditure,

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a repayment of an advance,

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a refund or repayment of an overpayment,

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} a rebate, including a tax rebate or some other price
  adjustment on a payment,

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} a reimbursement pursuant to a cost-sharing
  arrangement,

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} a recovery from an indemnification, or

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} a recovery under a claim for loss of or damage to a
  Crown asset

shall be credited to the appropriation against which the related
expenditure, advance or payment was charged.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 39
- 1991, c. 24, s. 16
:::

## [PART III.1]{.HLabel1}[Contracts]{.HTitleText1} {#h-228742 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Term of contract that money available

- **[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} It is
  a term of every contract providing for the payment of any money by Her
  Majesty that payment under that contract is subject to there being an
  appropriation for the particular service for the fiscal year in which
  any commitment under that contract would come in course of payment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Public opinion research

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} It is a term of every contract for public opinion
  research entered into by any person with Her Majesty that a written
  report will be provided by that person.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 40
- 1991, c. 24, s. 50(F)
- 2006, c. 9, s. 309
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-40-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 40"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Commitment

**[[40.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40.1 .sectionLabel}** The Government of
Canada is committed to taking appropriate measures to promote fairness,
openness and transparency in the bidding process for contracts with Her
Majesty for the performance of work, the supply of goods or the
rendering of services.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2006, c. 9, s. 310
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations respecting conditions under
which contracts awarded

- **[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may make regulations with respect to the
  conditions under which contracts may be entered into and,
  notwithstanding any other Act of Parliament,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} may direct that no contract by the terms of which
    payments are required in excess of such amount or amounts as the
    Governor in Council may prescribe shall be entered into or have any
    force or effect unless entry into the contract has been approved by
    the Governor in Council or the Treasury Board; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} may make regulations with respect to the security
    to be given to and in the name of Her Majesty to secure the due
    performance of contracts.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of Crown
  corporations, the Canada Revenue Agency or the Invest in Canada Hub.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 41
- 1991, c. 24, s. 50(F)
- 1999, c. 17, s. 160
- 2005, c. 38, s. 138
- 2006, c. 9, s. 311(F)
- 2017, c. 20, s. 445
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-41-20061212.html "Link to previous version of section 41"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- deemed terms of contracts

- **[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may make regulations fixing terms that are deemed
  to be expressly set out in contracts, or classes of contracts, that
  provide for the payment of any money by Her Majesty or a Crown
  corporation --- or in documents, or classes of documents, relating to
  such contracts and their formation --- including terms

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prohibiting payment of a contingency fee by any
    party to the contract to a person to whom the [Lobbying
    Act](/eng/acts/L-12.4) applies;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting corruption and collusion in the bidding
    process for contracts for the performance of work, the supply of
    goods or the rendering of services;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} requiring that a bidder on a contract for the
    performance of work, the supply of goods or the rendering of
    services make a declaration that the bidder has not committed an
    offence under section 121, 124 or 418 of the [Criminal
    Code](/eng/acts/C-46);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} respecting the provision of information or records
    to enable the Auditor General of Canada to inquire into the use of
    funds provided under funding agreements; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} requiring the public disclosure of basic
    information on contracts entered into with Her Majesty for the
    performance of work, the supply of goods or the rendering of
    services and having a value in excess of \$10,000.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of Auditor General

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Regulations made under subsection (1) may not
  infringe on the powers of the Auditor General under section 7.1 of the
  [Auditor General Act](/eng/acts/A-17).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- public opinion research

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, in respect of contracts
  for public opinion research, make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the form and content of the term of the
    contract and of the written report referred to in subsection 40(2);
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} requiring the report to be made available to the
    public in the manner, and subject to the conditions, specified in
    the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in this section.

  [[funding agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[funding agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a
      recipient, means an agreement in writing under which the recipient
      receives a grant, contribution or other funding from Her Majesty
      in right of Canada or a Crown corporation, either directly or
      through an agent or mandatary of Her Majesty, including by way of
      loan, but excludes contracts for the performance of work, the
      supply of goods or the rendering of services. ([accord de
      financement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[recipient]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[recipient]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual, body
      corporate, partnership or unincorporated organization that has, in
      any five consecutive fiscal years, received a total of one million
      dollars or more under one or more funding agreements, but does not
      include

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a Crown corporation;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a departmental corporation;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the government of a foreign state, a provincial
        government or a municipality, or any of their agencies;

      - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} a band, as defined in subsection 2(1) of the
        [Indian Act](/eng/acts/I-5), any member of the council or any
        agency of the band or an aboriginal body that is party to a
        self-government agreement given effect by an Act of Parliament
        or any of their agencies;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a corporation that is controlled by a
        municipality or a government other than the Government of
        Canada; or

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} an international
        organization. ([bénéficiaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 42
- 1991, c. 24, s. 17
- 2006, c. 9, ss. 312, 313
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-42-20061212.html "Link to previous version of section 42"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Five-year reviews

- **[[42.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to and except as otherwise
  provided in any directives issued by the Treasury Board, every
  department shall conduct a review every five years of the relevance
  and effectiveness of each ongoing program for which it is responsible.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[program]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[program]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  means a program of grants or contributions made to one or more
  recipients that are administered so as to achieve a common objective
  and for which spending authority is provided in an appropriation Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2006, c. 9, s. 260
:::

## [PART III.2]{.HLabel1}[Financial Transactions Related to Asset Management]{.HTitleText1} {#h-228796 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[42.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42.2 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[fiscal agent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[fiscal agent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fiscal agent appointed
    under this Part and includes the Bank of Canada. ([agent
    financier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[registrar]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[registrar]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a registrar appointed under
    this Part and includes the Bank of Canada. ([agent
    comptable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 2016, c. 12, s. 121
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Lending of funds

- **[[42.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, for the sound and
  efficient management of the Consolidated Revenue Fund, lend money by
  way of an auction on any terms and conditions that the Minister
  considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} However, the Minister is not to make a loan that
  exceeds the surplus of the part of the Consolidated Revenue Fund that
  is on deposit with the Bank of Canada at the time of the making of the
  loan.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2), the amount of the
  surplus is determined by the Bank of Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part of Consolidated Revenue Fund

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Any loans made under subsection (1) may only be paid
  out of the part of the Consolidated Revenue Fund that is on deposit
  with the Bank of Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers related to loans

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 42.5, the Minister may enter into any
  contract or agreement related to the loans and do any other thing
  relating to the loans that the Minister considers appropriate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2016, c. 12, s. 121
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Auctions

- **[[42.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42.4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the Minister lends money by way of an auction, the Minister may
  establish rules governing the conduct of the auction, including rules
  relating to any of the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the eligibility of persons to participate in the
    auction;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the provision to the Minister by participants of
    any information that the Minister considers relevant;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the form of bids;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the maximum amount that a participant may bid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rules not statutory instruments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Rules governing the conduct of an auction are not
  statutory instruments as defined in the [Statutory Instruments
  Act](/eng/acts/S-22).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2016, c. 12, s. 121
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Management of risks

**[[42.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42.5 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may authorize the Minister, subject to any terms and conditions
that the Governor in Council may specify, to enter into any contract or
agreement of a financial nature, including options, derivatives, swaps
and forwards, on any terms and conditions that the Minister considers
necessary for the management of risks related to the financial position
of the Government of Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2016, c. 12, s. 121
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registrars and fiscal agents

**[[42.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42.6 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} appoint one or more registrars or fiscal agents to
  perform any services that the Minister may specify in respect of
  financial transactions entered into under this Part; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} fix the remuneration or compensation of any registrar
  or fiscal agent appointed under this section.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2016, c. 12, s. 121
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment of related expenses

**[[42.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42.7 .sectionLabel}** With the
authorization of the Governor in Council, there may be paid out of the
Consolidated Revenue Fund

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the remuneration and compensation of registrars and
  fiscal agents appointed under section 42.6;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} all costs, expenses and charges incurred in the
  management of financial transactions referred to in this Part,
  including the negotiation, entering into and execution of those
  transactions;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} all money required to be paid under contracts and
  agreements entered into under subsection 42.3(5) or section 42.5;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} all money required to be paid under contracts and
  agreements entered into under this Act before the coming into force of
  this Part that could also have been entered into under this Part if
  this Part had been in force when these contracts and agreements were
  entered into; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} all money that the Minister considers appropriate to
  pay in the exercise of his or her power to do any other thing relating
  to the lending of money under subsection 42.3(5).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2016, c. 12, s. 121
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation

**[[42.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42.8 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may
delegate to any officer of the Department of Finance any of the
Minister's powers, duties or functions under this Part, except the power
to delegate under this section.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2016, c. 12, s. 121
:::

## [PART IV]{.HLabel1}[Public Debt]{.HTitleText1} {#h-228850 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Borrowing of money

- **[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding any statement in any
  other Act of Parliament to the effect that this Act or any portion or
  provision of it does not apply, no money shall be borrowed by or on
  behalf of Her Majesty in right of Canada except as provided by or
  under

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} this Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other Act of Parliament that expressly
    authorizes the borrowing of money; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any other Act of Parliament that provides for the
    borrowing of money from Her Majesty in right of Canada or of a
    province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuing of securities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No securities shall be issued by or on behalf of Her
  Majesty in right of Canada without the authority of Parliament.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 43
- 1999, c. 26, s. 21(E)
- 2001, c. 11, s. 4
:::

**[[43.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43.1 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2016, c.
7, s. 182\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-43.1-20071026.html "Link to previous version of section 43.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Raising of money

- **[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  by this Act or any other Act of Parliament authority is given to raise
  money by Her Majesty, the Governor in Council may, subject to the Act
  authorizing the raising of the money, authorize the Minister to borrow
  the money by any means that the Minister considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The aggregate principal amount of money borrowed by
  the Minister under this section in any fiscal year may not exceed the
  amount that is specified by order of the Governor in Council for that
  fiscal year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum --- exception

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Any money borrowed under section 47 is not to be
  taken into account for the purpose of calculating the aggregate
  principal amount of money borrowed by the Minister under this section
  in any fiscal year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of Minister

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any terms and conditions that the Governor
  in Council may specify, the Minister may enter into any contract or
  agreement, issue securities and do any other thing relating to the
  borrowing of money that the Minister considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of regulations and
  directions

  [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} Regulations and directions made under subsection
  41(1) do not apply in respect of contracts entered into under
  subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (3) applies with respect to anything done
  in relation to borrowings under this section, as it read immediately
  before the coming into force of this subsection, as though the
  borrowing were done under subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 44
- 1999, c. 26, s. 22
- [2020, c. 5, s. 23](#1240825-1241702){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1240825-1241702.html"}
- [2024, c. 17, s. 394](#1468484-1483770){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1468484-1483770.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-44-20200325.html "Link to previous version of section 44"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Auctions

- **[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the
  Minister borrows money by way of an auction, the Minister may
  establish rules governing the conduct of the auction, including rules
  relating to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the eligibility of persons to participate in the
    auction;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the provision to the Minister by participants of
    any information that the Minister considers relevant, including
    information respecting holdings of securities and transactions in
    securities;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the form of bids;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the maximum amount that may be bid for by a
    participant; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the certification and verification of bids.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rules not statutory instruments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Rules governing the conduct of an auction are not
  statutory instruments as defined in the [Statutory Instruments
  Act](/eng/acts/S-22).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 45
- 1999, c. 26, s. 22
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contracts and agreements

**[[45.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45.1 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may authorize the Minister, subject to any terms and conditions
that the Governor in Council may specify, to enter into any contract or
agreement of a financial nature, including options, derivatives, swaps
and forwards, on such terms and conditions as the Minister considers
necessary.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 24, s. 18
- 1999, c. 26, s. 22
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers --- management of assets and
liabilities

**[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may, on any
terms and conditions the Minister considers appropriate, do any of the
following if the Minister considers it appropriate for the sound and
efficient management of the assets and liabilities of Canada, including
contingent liabilities:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} purchase or acquire securities of Canada or any other
  securities, including purchasing or acquiring them on their issuance,
  and hold, lend or sell securities of Canada or any other securities;
  and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} create a charge on, or right or interest in,
  securities of Canada or any other securities held by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 46
- 1999, c. 26, s. 22
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authority to borrow money

**[[46.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46.1 .sectionLabel}** In any fiscal year,
the Governor in Council may by order authorize the Minister to borrow
money for

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the payment of any amount that is required to be paid
  in that fiscal year in respect of any money borrowed under the
  authority of this Act or any other Act of Parliament;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the extinguishment or reduction of any liability of
  Canada, if the Minister is of the opinion that the liability should be
  extinguished or reduced; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the payment, by Her Majesty, of any amount in
  extraordinary circumstances, including in the event of a natural
  disaster or to promote the stability or maintain the efficiency of the
  financial system in Canada, if the Minister is of the opinion that the
  borrowing of money is necessary in those circumstances.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1999, c. 26, s. 22
- 2007, c. 29, s. 86
- 2016, c. 7, s. 183
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-46.1-20071026.html "Link to previous version of section 46.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

**[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** During the period
beginning on the day on which this section comes into force and ending
on September 30, 2020, the Minister may borrow money for

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the payment of any amount that is required to be paid
  in that period in respect of any money borrowed under the authority of
  this Act or any other Act of Parliament; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the payment, by Her Majesty, of any amount in
  extraordinary circumstances, including in the event of a natural
  disaster or to promote the stability or maintain the efficiency of the
  financial system in Canada, if the Minister is of the opinion that the
  borrowing of money is necessary in those circumstances.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 47
- 2007, c. 29, s. 86
- [2020, c. 5, s. 24](#1240827-1241709){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1240827-1241709.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-47-20071026.html "Link to previous version of section 47"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Currency of borrowings

- **[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  amounts raised by way of loan under the authority of this Act or any
  other Act of Parliament in the currency or currencies of any country
  or countries may be repaid in the currency or currencies of any
  country or countries and securities issued under the authority of this
  Act or any other Act of Parliament in the currency or currencies of
  any country or countries may be made payable in the currency or
  currencies of any country or countries.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculation of amount in foreign
  currency

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where an Act of Parliament, whether enacted before or
  after the coming into force of this subsection, authorizes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the raising of a specific or maximum number of
    dollars by way of loan or the issue of securities, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the guarantee of the payment of a liability or
    obligation for a specific or maximum number of dollars,

  the authorized transaction may be undertaken, in whole or in part, in
  the currency of a country other than Canada, and for that purpose the
  specific or maximum number of dollars shall be construed as an
  equivalent amount in the currency of the other country, calculated at
  the daily average rate of exchange between the Canadian dollar and the
  currency concerned quoted by the Bank of Canada on the day immediately
  preceding the day on which the money is borrowed, the proceeds from
  the issue of securities are received or the guarantee is given, as the
  case may be, or at any other rate of exchange in use between those
  currencies that the Minister considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculation where limitation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of any limitation in respect of an
  amount

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that may be borrowed,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for which securities may be issued, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the payment of which may be guaranteed under this
    Act or any other Act of Parliament,

  the principal amount borrowed or authorized to be borrowed, the
  principal amount of any securities previously issued or authorized to
  be issued or the amount guaranteed or authorized to be guaranteed, as
  the case may be, payable in a currency of any country other than
  Canada, shall be deemed to be the Canadian dollar equivalent of the
  value thereof, as calculated under subsection (2), regardless of any
  premium or discount at which the securities were sold and regardless
  of any premium that may be payable on early redemption.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 48
- 1991, c. 24, ss. 19, 50(F)
- 1999, c. 31, s. 110(F)
- 2017, c. 20, s. 104
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-48-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 48"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report on debt management

- **[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} After
  the Public Accounts are tabled in the House of Commons, the Minister
  shall cause to be tabled in each House of Parliament, within the first
  30 days on which that House is sitting after the Public Accounts are
  tabled in the House of Commons, a report on the activities of the
  Minister in relation to the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the money borrowed in the fiscal year to which the
    Public Accounts relate;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} the money that is borrowed under an order made
    under paragraph 46.1(c) of the [Financial Administration
    Act](/eng/acts/F-11), other than that borrowed under such an order
    made during the period beginning on March 23, 2021 and ending on May
    6, 2021, and that is due at the end of the fiscal year to which the
    Public Accounts relate; and

  - [(a.2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2021, c. 7, s. 19\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the management of the public debt in the fiscal
    year to which the Public Accounts relate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report on Minister's plans

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall cause to be tabled in each House
  of Parliament for every fiscal year, not later than the 30th day on
  which that House is sitting after the start of the fiscal year to
  which the report relates, a report on the Minister's plans in relation
  to the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the money to be borrowed in that fiscal year and
    the purposes for which the moneys will be borrowed; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the management of the public debt in that fiscal
    year.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 49
- 1999, c. 26, s. 23
- 2007, c. 29, s. 87
- 2016, c. 7, s. 184
- [2020, c. 5, s. 25](#1240829-1241716){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1240829-1241716.html"}
- [2021, c. 7, s. 19](#1292605-1292683){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1292605-1292683.html"}
- [2022, c. 10, s. 185](#1357017-1366552){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357017-1366552.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-49-20210506.html "Link to previous version of section 49"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report --- borrowings in respect of
extraordinary circumstances

- **[[49.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall cause to be
  tabled in each House of Parliament a report on the money borrowed or
  to be borrowed under an order made under paragraph 46.1(c) within the
  first 30 days on which that House is sitting after the day on which
  the Governor in Council's authorization is given under that paragraph.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report --- borrowings in respect of
  extraordinary circumstances

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall cause to be tabled in each House
  of Parliament a report on the money borrowed or to be borrowed under
  paragraph 47(b) within the first 30 days on which that House is
  sitting after the day on which the Minister first borrows money under
  that paragraph.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2016, c. 7, s. 185
- [2020, c. 5, s. 26](#1240836-1241722){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1240836-1241722.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-49.1-20171123.html "Link to previous version of section 49.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Signing securities

- **[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Security certificates evidencing
  securities issued under the authority of this Part shall be signed by
  the Deputy Minister of Finance or an officer of the Department of
  Finance designated by the Governor in Council to sign on behalf of the
  Deputy Minister of Finance, and shall be countersigned by such officer
  of the Department of Finance or other person as the Governor in
  Council designates for that purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Facsimile signatures

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may direct that there be substituted for
  signatures in the proper handwriting of one or both of the persons
  authorized to sign or countersign security certificates under
  subsection (1), facsimiles of those signatures.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 50
- 1995, c. 17, s. 59
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registrars and fiscal agents

- **[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} appoint one or more registrars to perform such
    services in respect of the registration of loans as the Governor in
    Council may prescribe;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} appoint one or more fiscal agents to perform such
    services in respect of loans as the Governor in Council may
    prescribe; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} fix the remuneration or compensation of any
    registrar or fiscal agent appointed under this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation to Minister

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may authorize the Minister to
  do anything referred to in paragraphs (1)(a) to (c) for any period
  that the Governor in Council considers appropriate.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 51
- 1999, c. 26, s. 23.1
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Records of money borrowed

- **[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister shall cause to be maintained a system of books and records

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} showing all money authorized by Parliament to be
    borrowed by the issue and sale of securities;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} containing a description and record of all money so
    borrowed and securities issued; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} showing all amounts paid in respect of the
    principal of or interest on all money so borrowed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Accounting by fiscal agents and
  registrars

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every fiscal agent and registrar shall annually, and
  as often as required by the Minister, give to the Minister an
  accounting, in such form and terms and containing such information as
  the Minister prescribes, of all his transactions as fiscal agent or
  registrar.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. F-10, s. 43
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sinking fund

**[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may provide for the creation and management of a sinking fund with
respect to any issue of securities or with respect to all securities
issued.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. F-10, s. 44
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Borrowed money and interest

**[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54 .sectionLabel}** The repayment of all
money borrowed and interest on that money, including the principal of
and interest on all securities issued by or on behalf of Her Majesty
with the authority of Parliament, is a charge on and payable out of the
Consolidated Revenue Fund.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 54
- 2007, c. 29, s. 88
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-54-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 54"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment of loan expenses

**[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** With the authority of the
Governor in Council, there may be paid out of the Consolidated Revenue
Fund

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} all money required under section 53 to provide a
  sinking fund or other means of securing repayment of securities;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the remuneration and compensation of registrars and
  fiscal agents appointed under section 51;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} all costs, expenses and charges incurred in the
  negotiation or raising of loans or in the issue, redemption,
  servicing, payment and management of any loan and any securities
  issued in respect thereof;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} all money required to be paid under contracts and
  agreements entered into under this Part, either before or after the
  coming into force of this paragraph; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} all money that the Minister considers appropriate to
  pay in the exercise of his or her power to do any other thing relating
  to the borrowing of money under subsection 44(3).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 55
- 1991, c. 24, s. 50(F)
- 1999, c. 26, s. 23.2
- 2016, c. 12, s. 122
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-55-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 55"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment for securities to agent or by
salary deduction in trust money

- **[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  it is provided by a prospectus or other official notice issued by or
  under the authority of the Minister that a subscriber may purchase
  securities by payments to an authorized agent or by deductions from
  the remuneration of the subscriber by his employer, the amount of any
  such payment or deduction that has not been accounted for by the
  delivery of securities to the subscriber or repaid to the subscriber
  shall be deemed to be money received in trust for Her Majesty by the
  agent or employer for which the agent or employer is accountable to
  Her Majesty under section 76.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount deemed segregated in trust

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where money paid or deducted pursuant to
  subsection (1) cannot be identified among the assets of the employer
  or agent, a portion of those assets equal in value to the amount of
  the payment or deduction shall be deemed to be segregated and held in
  trust for Her Majesty.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 56
- 1991, c. 24, s. 50(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Investors' Indemnity Account

**[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** There shall be
established in the accounts of Canada an account to be known as the
Investors' Indemnity Account to which shall be credited the sum of
twenty-five thousand dollars, such further amounts as are appropriated
by Parliament for the purposes of this section and any recoveries of the
losses referred to in section 58.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. F-10, s. 48
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment of losses

**[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may, in
accordance with and subject to the regulations, pay out of the
Investors' Indemnity Account any losses sustained by subscribers for
securities who have paid all or part of the purchase price for those
securities but have not received the security or repayment of the amount
so paid, and losses sustained by any person in the redemption of
securities.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. F-10, s. 49
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Not bound to execute trusts

**[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** Her Majesty and a fiscal
agent or registrar acting as such are not bound to see to the execution
of any express or implied trust to which any securities are subject.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. F-10, s. 50
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may make such regulations as he deems necessary to
  provide for the management of the public debt of Canada and the
  payment of interest thereon and, without limiting the generality of
  the foregoing, may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the inscription of security certificates and
    the registration of securities and prescribing the effect of the
    inscription or registration;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the transmission, transfer, redemption and
    cancellation of securities and the exchange and destruction of any
    security certificates, and, without limiting the generality of the
    foregoing,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for the transmission, transfer or redemption of
      securities pursuant to a judgment or as the result of the death,
      dissolution or bankruptcy of the registered owner of the
      securities, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the conditions on which the transfer
      and redemption of securities or the exchange of security
      certificates registered in the names of infants, minors or other
      persons not of full capacity to enter into ordinary contracts may
      be made;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for the issue of security certificates or making of
    payments in respect of damaged, lost, stolen or destroyed security
    certificates or interest coupons, and of the cheques pertaining
    thereto and prescribing conditions to the issue or payment;

  - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} respecting the issuance and holding of
    non-certificated securities;

  - [(c.2)]{.lawlabel} respecting the circumstances under which the
    beneficial owner of a non-certificated security can obtain a
    security certificate or under which the beneficial owner of a
    security certificate can obtain a non-certificated security;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} requiring guarantees to be given to the registrar
    in such manner and by such persons as the regulations may prescribe,
    before the registrar is authorized to make any entry in the
    register;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} authorizing the correction by the registrar, in
    such circumstances as may be prescribed by the regulations, of
    errors in the register and otherwise authorizing rectification of
    the register;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} providing for the payment of losses out of the
    Investors' Indemnity Account;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} deeming a specified transaction or a transaction of
    a specified class, including the issuance of securities, to be a
    transaction to borrow money for the purposes of subsection 43(1);
    and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} notwithstanding any right provided by or under any
    other Act of Parliament to borrow money without the Minister's
    authorization, requiring the Minister's authorization in respect of
    a specified transaction to borrow money or a transaction of a
    specified class to borrow money.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of register

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The register maintained pursuant to subsection (1)
  may be in a bound or loose-leaf form or in a photographic film form or
  may be maintained by any system of mechanical or electronic data
  processing or any other information storage device that is capable of
  reproducing any required information in intelligible written form
  within a reasonable time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Canada Evidence Act](/eng/acts/C-5)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The register maintained pursuant to subsection (1) is
  deemed to be a record for the purposes of the [Canada Evidence
  Act](/eng/acts/C-5) and every employee of the Bank of Canada who
  supervises the inscription or registration of securities in the
  register is deemed to be a manager of the Bank of Canada for the
  purposes of that Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's authorization

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a regulation is made under paragraph (1)(g) or
  (h), the Minister may authorize, subject to any terms and conditions
  that the Minister considers appropriate,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the specified transaction;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a particular transaction named by the Minister
    within the specified class;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} transactions of a particular subclass described by
    the Minister within the specified class; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} transactions of the specified class.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 60
- 1995, c. 17, s. 60
- 2001, c. 11, s. 5
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation

**[[60.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60.1 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may
delegate to any officer of the Department of Finance any of the powers,
duties and functions of the Minister under this Part, except the power
to delegate under this section.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1999, c. 26, s. 24
:::

## [PART IV.1]{.HLabel1}[Stability and Efficiency of the Financial System]{.HTitleText1} {#h-229029 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[60.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in
  this Part.

  [[debt obligation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[debt obligation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a bond, debenture,
      note or other evidence of indebtedness of an entity, whether
      secured or unsecured. ([titre de créance]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity, including a trust,
      that, in the Minister's opinion, is operating in
      Canada. ([entité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[financial markets]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[financial markets]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes markets for
      money, bonds, equities, derivatives, foreign exchange and
      commodities. ([marchés financiers]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[financial system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[financial system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes financial
      institutions, financial markets and payment systems as defined in
      section 36 of the [Canadian Payments
      Act](/eng/acts/C-21). ([système financier]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a corporation, a share, a class
        of shares or a debt obligation of the corporation, and includes
        any conversion or exchange privilege, option or other right to
        acquire a share of the corporation; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in relation to any other entity, any ownership
        interest in or debt obligation of the
        entity. ([titre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contracts

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), the Minister may, with the
  Governor in Council's authorization, enter into, on behalf of Her
  Majesty in right of Canada, any contract that in the Minister's
  opinion is necessary to promote the stability or maintain the
  efficiency of the financial system in Canada, including such a
  contract to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} purchase, acquire, hold, lend or sell or otherwise
    dispose of securities of an entity;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} create a charge on, or right or interest in,
    securities of an entity held by the Minister;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} make a loan to an entity;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} provide a line of credit to an entity;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} guarantee any debt, obligation or financial asset
    of an entity; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} provide loan insurance or credit insurance for the
    benefit of an entity in respect of any debt, obligation or financial
    asset of the entity.

- [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2020, c. 6, s. 8\]]{.Repealed}

- [(2.2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2020, c. 6, s. 8\]]{.Repealed}

- [(2.3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2020, c. 6, s. 8\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application to certain entities

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (2)(a) does not apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} shares, as defined in subsection 973.2(15) of the
    [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01), of a bank or bank holding company, as
    defined in section 2 of that Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} shares, as defined in subsection 459.9(14) of the
    [Cooperative Credit Associations Act](/eng/acts/C-41.01), of an
    association as defined in section 2 of that Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} shares, as defined in subsection 1016.7(15) of the
    [Insurance Companies Act](/eng/acts/I-11.8), of a company or
    insurance holding company, as defined in subsection 2(1) of that
    Act; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} shares, as defined in subsection 527.9(15) of the
    [Trust and Loan Companies Act](/eng/acts/T-19.8), of a company as
    defined in section 2 of that Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Section 90 does not apply

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Section 90 does not apply if the Minister purchases,
  acquires or sells or otherwise disposes, under paragraph (2)(a), of
  shares within the meaning of that section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Section 61 and [Surplus Crown Assets
  Act](/eng/acts/S-27) do not apply

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Section 61 and the [Surplus Crown Assets
  Act](/eng/acts/S-27) do not apply if the Minister holds, loans or
  sells or otherwise disposes of securities under paragraph (2)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payments out of C.R.F.

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Any amount payable under or in connection with a
  contract entered into under this section may be paid out of the
  Consolidated Revenue Fund, on the requisition of the Minister, at the
  times and in the manner that the Minister considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retroactive effect

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} This section applies to any contract entered into on
  or after November 30, 2008.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 2, s. 232
- [2020, c. 5, s. 27](#1240838-1241748){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1240838-1241748.html"}
- [2020, c. 6, s. 8](#1243243-1261814){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1243243-1261814.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-60.2-20200325.html "Link to previous version of section 60.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporation

- **[[60.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If, in the Minister's opinion, it is
  necessary to promote the stability or maintain the efficiency of the
  financial system in Canada, the Minister may, during the period
  beginning on the day on which this subsection comes into force and
  ending on September 30, 2020, with the Governor in Council's
  authorization, procure the incorporation of a corporation, all of the
  shares of which are held by the Minister on behalf of Her Majesty in
  right of Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Not agent of Her Majesty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The corporation is not an agent of Her Majesty in
  right of Canada unless it is declared to be an agent of Her Majesty
  under an Act of Parliament.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of Part X

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any regulations made under subsection (4),
  Part X does not apply to the corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may make regulations respecting the
  governance of the corporation, including regulations that adapt any
  provisions of this Act or the [Canada Business Corporations
  Act](/eng/acts/C-44) and any regulations made under those Acts for the
  purpose of applying those provisions as adapted to the corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Directives

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may give a directive to the corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Implementation

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The directors of the corporation shall ensure that a
  directive is implemented in a prompt and efficient manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Best interests

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Compliance by the corporation with a directive is
  deemed to be in the best interests of the corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms and conditions

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, by order, establish terms and
  conditions in accordance with which the corporation shall enter into
  financial transactions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contract with Her Majesty

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} The corporation may enter into a contract with Her
  Majesty in right of Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payments out of C.R.F.

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may make payments to the corporation
  out of the Consolidated Revenue Fund, at the times and in the manner
  that the Minister considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Loans to corporation

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, out of the Consolidated Revenue
  Fund, lend money to the corporation on any terms and conditions that
  the Minister may fix.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of Minister

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may merge, sell, wind-up or dissolve
  the corporation, dispose of any or all shares of the corporation or
  take other similar measures in respect of the corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Statutory Instruments
  Act](/eng/acts/S-22)

  [(13)]{.lawlabel} The [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22) does
  not apply to a directive given under subsection (5) or to an order
  made under subsection (8).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication in [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/)

  [(14)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall publish a directive given under
  subsection (5) or an order made under subsection (8) in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/).

::: HistoricalNote
- [2020, c. 5, s. 28](#1240849-1241779){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1240849-1241779.html"}
- [2020, c. 6, s. 9](#1243250-1244309){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1243250-1244309.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-60.3-20200325.html "Link to previous version of section 60.3"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entity other than corporation

- **[[60.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If, in the Minister's opinion, it is
  necessary to promote the stability or maintain the efficiency of the
  financial system in Canada, the Minister may, during the period
  beginning on the day on which this subsection comes into force and
  ending on September 30, 2020, with the Governor in Council's
  authorization, establish an entity, other than a corporation, on any
  terms and conditions that the Minister considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payments out of C.R.F.

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may make payments to the entity out of
  the Consolidated Revenue Fund, at the times and in the manner that the
  Minister considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Loans to entity

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, out of the Consolidated Revenue
  Fund, lend money to the entity on any terms and conditions that the
  Minister may fix.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2020, c. 5, s. 28](#1240849-1241788){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1240849-1241788.html"}
- [2020, c. 6, s. 10](#1243252-1244311){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1243252-1244311.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-60.4-20200325.html "Link to previous version of section 60.4"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART V]{.HLabel1}[Public Property]{.HTitleText1} {#h-229064 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transfers, etc., of public property

- **[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any other Act of
  Parliament, no transfer, lease or loan of public property shall be
  made except under the [Federal Real Property and Federal Immovables
  Act](/eng/acts/F-8.4) in the case of federal real property or a
  federal immovable as defined in that Act, or under subsection (2) in
  the case of other public property.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council, on the recommendation of the
  Treasury Board, may authorize or make regulations authorizing the
  transfer, lease or loan of public property other than federal real
  property and federal immovables as defined in the [Federal Real
  Property and Federal Immovables Act](/eng/acts/F-8.4).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 61
- 1991, c. 50, s. 27
- 2001, c. 4, s. 160
- 2015, c. 3, s. 93(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-61-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 61"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Management of public property

**[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** The deputy head of every
department shall maintain adequate records in relation to public
property for which the department is responsible and shall comply with
regulations of the Treasury Board governing the custody and control of
public property.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. F-10, s. 53
:::

## [PART VI]{.HLabel1}[Public Accounts]{.HTitleText1} {#h-229076 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Accounts of Canada

- **[[63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to regulations of the
  Treasury Board, the Receiver General shall cause accounts to be kept
  in such manner as to show

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the expenditures made under each appropriation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the revenues of Canada; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the other payments into and out of the Consolidated
    Revenue Fund.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assets and liabilities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Receiver General shall cause accounts to be kept
  to show such of the assets and direct and contingent liabilities of
  Canada and shall establish such reserves with respect to the assets
  and liabilities as, in the opinion of the President of the Treasury
  Board and the Minister, are required to present fairly the financial
  position of Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Accounts in Canadian currency

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The accounts of Canada shall be kept in the currency
  of Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 63
- 1999, c. 31, s. 111(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Submission of Public Accounts to
Parliament

- **[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  report, called the Public Accounts, shall be prepared by the Receiver
  General for each fiscal year and shall be laid before the House of
  Commons by the President of the Treasury Board on or before December
  31 next following the end of that fiscal year or, if the House of
  Commons is not then sitting, on any of the first fifteen days next
  thereafter that the House of Commons is sitting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of Public Accounts

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Public Accounts shall be in such form as the
  President of the Treasury Board and the Minister may direct, and shall
  include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statement of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the financial transactions of the fiscal year,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the expenditures and revenues of Canada for the
      fiscal year, and
