    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} such of the assets and liabilities of Canada
      as, in the opinion of the President of the Treasury Board and the
      Minister, are required to show the financial position of Canada as
      at the termination of the fiscal year;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the contingent liabilities of Canada;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the opinion of the Auditor General of Canada as
    required under section 6 of the [Auditor General
    Act](/eng/acts/A-17); and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} such other accounts and information relating to the
    fiscal year as are deemed necessary by the President of the Treasury
    Board and the Minister to present fairly the financial transactions
    and the financial position of Canada or as are required by this Act
    or any other Act of Parliament to be shown in the Public Accounts.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 64
- 1999, c. 31, s. 112(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ministers to provide records, etc.

**[[65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of the
keeping of the accounts of Canada under section 63 and the preparation
of the Public Accounts under section 64, the Receiver General may, from
time to time, subject to such regulations as the Treasury Board may
make, send a notice to each appropriate Minister requesting such
records, accounts or statements or other information as is specified in
the notice and each appropriate Minister shall, within such reasonable
time as is specified in the notice, provide the Receiver General with
the records, accounts or statements or other information requested.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. F-10, s. 56
- R.S., c. 11(2nd Supp.), s. 1
- 1976-77, c. 34, s. 23
- 1980-81-82-83, c. 170, s. 16
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quarterly financial reports

- **[[65.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every department shall cause to be
  prepared, in the form and manner provided for by the Treasury Board, a
  quarterly financial report for each of the first three fiscal quarters
  of each fiscal year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The report shall contain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a financial statement for the fiscal quarter and
    the period from the start of the fiscal year to the end of that
    fiscal quarter;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} comparative financial information for the preceding
    fiscal year; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a statement outlining the results, risks and
    significant changes in relation to operations, personnel and
    programs.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report to be made public

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The appropriate Minister shall cause the report to be
  made public within 60 days after the end of the fiscal quarter to
  which the report relates.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Treasury Board may, by regulation, exempt a
  department from the requirement set out in subsection (1) or provide
  that any of the content referred to in subsection (2) be excluded from
  its report.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 31, s. 58
:::

## [PART VII]{.HLabel1}[Assignment of Crown Debts]{.HTitleText1} {#h-229121 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[66]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,

[[appropriate paying officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[appropriate paying officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a
    Crown debt, means the paying officer who makes the payments in
    respect of that debt; ([agent payeur compétent]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[contract]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[contract]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a contract involving the
    payment of money by the Crown; ([marché]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[Crown]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Crown]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means Her Majesty in right of
    Canada; ([Sa Majesté]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Crown debt]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Crown debt]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any existing or future debt
    due or becoming due by the Crown, and any other chose in action in
    respect of which there is a right of recovery enforceable by action
    against the Crown; ([créance sur Sa Majesté]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[paying officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[paying officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any person designated
    as such by regulation; ([agent payeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means prescribed by
    regulation. ([Version anglaise seulement]{.DefinitionOtherLangOnly})

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 66
- 1999, c. 31, s. 113(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}General prohibition

**[[67]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67 .sectionLabel}** Except as provided in
this Act or any other Act of Parliament,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a Crown debt is not assignable; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} no transaction purporting to be an assignment of a
  Crown debt is effective so as to confer on any person any rights or
  remedies in respect of that debt.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. F-10, s. 80
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assignments of specified Crown debts

- **[[68]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this section, an
  assignment may be made of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a Crown debt that is an amount due or becoming due
    under a contract; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other Crown debt of a prescribed class.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for validity

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The assignment referred to in subsection (1) is valid
  only if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is absolute, in writing and made under the hand
    of the assignor;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it does not purport to be by way of charge only;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} notice of the assignment has been given to the
    Crown as provided in section 69.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of assignment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The assignment referred to in subsections (1) and (2)
  is effectual in law, subject to all equities that would have been
  entitled to priority over the right of the assignee if this section
  had not been enacted, to pass and transfer, from the date service on
  the Crown of notice of the assignment is effected,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the legal right to the Crown debt;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all legal and other remedies for the Crown debt;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the power to give a good discharge for the Crown
    debt without the concurrence of the assignor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Original conditions and restrictions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An assignment made in accordance with this Part is
  subject to all conditions and restrictions in respect of the right of
  transfer that relate to the original Crown debt or that attach to or
  are contained in the original contract.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Salary, wages, pay and allowances not
  assignable

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (1), any amount due or
  becoming due by the Crown as or on account of salary, wages, pay or
  pay and allowances is not assignable and no transaction purporting to
  be an assignment of any such amount is effective to confer on any
  person any rights or remedies in respect of that amount.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 68
- 1991, c. 24, s. 49(E)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of assignment

- **[[69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  notice referred to in paragraph 68(2)(c) shall be given to the Crown
  by serving on or sending by registered mail to the Receiver General or
  a paying officer, in prescribed form, notice of the assignment,
  together with a copy of the assignment accompanied by such other
  documents completed in such manner as may be prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When notice deemed served

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Service of the notice referred to in subsection (1)
  shall be deemed not to have been effected until acknowledgment of the
  notice, in prescribed form, is sent to the assignee, by registered
  mail, under the hand of the appropriate paying officer.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. F-10, s. 82
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation of application of this Part

**[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70 .sectionLabel}** This Part does not apply

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} to any negotiable instrument;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} to any Crown debt incurred by or in the name of a
  corporation set out in Schedule III; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} to any securities issued under Part IV.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 70
- 1998, c. 13, s. 21
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} designating persons as paying officers for the
  purposes of this Part;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing additional classes of Crown debts for the
  purpose of subsection 68(1);

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the forms of notices of assignment and
  acknowledgments thereof;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the documents to be submitted in
  connection with a notice of assignment, the forms of those documents
  and the manner in which they are to be completed; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} generally, for carrying into effect the purposes and
  provisions of this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. F-10, s. 84
:::

## [PART VIII]{.HLabel1}[Assignment of Debts Due to the Crown Under Payment Bonds]{.HTitleText1} {#h-229182 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,

[[Crown]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Crown]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means Her Majesty in right of Canada
    or any agent of Her Majesty in right of Canada and includes a Crown
    corporation and a departmental corporation; ([Sa
    Majesté]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[payment bond]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[payment bond]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a bond held by the Crown
    as security for the payment of certain classes of persons who
    perform labour or services or supply material in connection with a
    contract between the Crown and a
    contractor. ([cautionnement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 72
- 1991, c. 24, s. 50(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assignment of Crown right under payment
bond

- **[[73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  an amount is due to the Crown under the provisions of a payment bond,
  a person who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} performed labour or services or supplied material
    in connection with the contract in respect of which the payment bond
    is held,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is within a class of persons for the payment of
    which the payment bond is held as security, and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has not been paid in full for the labour or
    services performed or material supplied by him in connection with
    the contract within the time provided in the payment bond for
    payment to the class of persons of which that person is a member,

  is, without any act by or notice by or to the Crown, an assignee of
  the right of the Crown to recover an amount under the payment bond
  determined pursuant to subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount recoverable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amount that may be recovered by a person referred
  to in subsection (1) shall be equal to the lesser of the amount due to
  that person for the labour or services performed or material supplied
  by him under the contract and the amount due to the Crown under the
  provisions of the payment bond.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Crown not a party to action

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person who is an assignee of the right of the Crown
  to recover an amount under a payment bond may, in his own name,
  exercise the right that, but for this Act, the Crown would have had to
  bring action to enforce payment under the payment bond in accordance
  with its terms and conditions and the Crown shall be neither a party
  to, nor liable for any costs in connection with, that action.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 73
- 1991, c. 24, s. 50(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of certified copy of payment
bond

- **[[74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-74 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A copy
  of a payment bond certified by the officer of the Crown having custody
  of the original payment bond shall be provided by that officer to any
  person who files with him an affidavit setting out that the person has
  performed labour or services or supplied material in connection with
  the contract for which the bond is held and that he has not been paid
  in full therefor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certified copy as evidence

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A document purporting to be a copy of a payment bond
  certified by the officer of the Crown having custody of the original
  payment bond is, without proof of the signature of the officer,
  admissible in evidence in any court of justice, or before a person
  having by law or by consent of parties authority to hear, receive and
  examine evidence in any action taken by a person under this Part, and
  has the same probative force as the original document would have if it
  were proven in the ordinary way.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 74
- 1991, c. 24, s. 50(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[75]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council,
on the recommendation of the Treasury Board, may make regulations for
carrying into effect the purposes and provisions of this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. F-10, s. 88
:::

## [PART IX]{.HLabel1}[Civil Liability and Offences]{.HTitleText1} {#h-229213 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to persons failing to pay over
public money

- **[[76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  the appropriate Minister or the Receiver General believes on
  reasonable grounds that any person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has received money for Her Majesty and has not duly
    paid it over,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has received money for which the person is
    accountable to Her Majesty and has not duly accounted for it, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has received any public money applicable to any
    purpose and has not duly applied it,

  the appropriate Minister or the Receiver General, as the case may be,
  may cause a notice to be served on that person, or on the person's
  representative in case of the person's death, requiring the person,
  within such time after the service of the notice as may be named
  therein, duly to pay over, account for or apply that money, as the
  case may be, and to transmit to the appropriate Minister or the
  Receiver General, as the notice provides, proper vouchers that the
  person has done so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proceedings where notice not complied
  with

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a person does not comply with a notice served
  under subsection (1), the appropriate Minister or the Receiver
  General, as the case may be, shall state an account between that
  person and Her Majesty showing the amount of money not duly paid over,
  accounted for or applied, as the case may be, and may charge interest
  on the whole or any part of that amount from such date as the
  appropriate Minister or the Receiver General may determine and at such
  rate as may be prescribed pursuant to subsection 155.1(6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Evidence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In any proceedings for the recovery of money referred
  to in subsection (2), a copy of the account stated and certified by
  the appropriate Minister or the Receiver General is evidence that the
  amount stated in the account, together with interest, is due and
  payable to Her Majesty, without proof of the signature of the
  appropriate Minister or the Receiver General or the official character
  of the office.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recovery

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Any amount of money referred to in subsection (1) and
  the interest on that amount may be recovered as a debt due to Her
  Majesty.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 76
- 1991, c. 24, ss. 20, 50(F)
- 1999, c. 31, s. 114(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Evidence

**[[77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77 .sectionLabel}** Where it appears by the
books or accounts kept by or in the office of any person employed in the
collection or management of the revenue, in any accounting by that
person or by his written acknowledgment or confession, that that person
has, by virtue of his office or employment, received money belonging to
Her Majesty and refused or neglected to pay over that money to the
proper persons at the proper times, an affidavit deposing to those
facts, taken by any person having knowledge thereof, shall, in any
proceedings for the recovery of that money, be admitted in evidence and
is, in the absence of any evidence to the contrary, proof of the facts
stated therein.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. F-10, s. 90
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability for loss

**[[78]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78 .sectionLabel}** Where, by reason of any
malfeasance or negligence by any person employed in collecting or
receiving any public money, any sum of money is lost to Her Majesty,
that person is accountable for the sum as if that person had collected
and received it and it may be recovered from that person as if that
person had collected and received it.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. F-10, s. 91
- 1980-81-82-83, c. 170, s. 19
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations in respect of losses of money
and public property

**[[79]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council,
on the recommendation of the Treasury Board, may make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the actions to be taken in respect of
  losses of money or public property, however caused, suffered by Her
  Majesty;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the charging of losses of money suffered
  by Her Majesty against the appropriations to which they relate; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the records to be kept and providing for
  the reporting in the Public Accounts in respect of every loss referred
  to in paragraph (a).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1980-81-82-83, c. 170, s. 19
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offences and punishment

- **[[80]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-80 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  officer or person acting in any office or employment connected with
  the collection, management or disbursement of public money who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} receives any compensation or reward for the
    performance of any official duty, except as by law prescribed,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} conspires or colludes with any other person to
    defraud Her Majesty, or makes opportunity for any person to defraud
    Her Majesty,

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} designedly permits any contravention of the law by
    any other person,

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} wilfully makes or signs any false entry in any
    book, or wilfully makes or signs any false certificate or return in
    any case in which it is the duty of that officer or person to make
    an entry, certificate or return,

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} having knowledge or information of the
    contravention of this Act or the regulations or any revenue law of
    Canada by any person, or of fraud committed by any person against
    Her Majesty, under this Act or the regulations or any revenue law of
    Canada, fails to report, in writing, that knowledge or information
    to a superior officer, or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} demands or accepts or attempts to collect, directly
    or indirectly, as payment or gift or otherwise, any sum of money, or
    other thing of value, for the compromise, adjustment or settlement
    of any charge or complaint for any contravention or alleged
    contravention of law,

  is guilty of an indictable offence and liable on conviction to a fine
  not exceeding five thousand dollars and to imprisonment for a term not
  exceeding five years.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fraud against Her Majesty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every officer or person acting in any office or
  employment connected with the collection, management or disbursement
  of public money who, by deceit, falsehood or other fraudulent means,
  defrauds Her Majesty of any money, securities, property or service is
  guilty of an indictable offence and liable on conviction,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the amount of the money or the value of the
    securities, property or service does not exceed \$5,000, to a fine
    not exceeding \$5,000 and to imprisonment for a term not exceeding
    five years; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the amount of the money or the value of the
    securities, property or service exceeds \$5,000, to a fine not
    exceeding that amount or that value and to imprisonment for a term
    not exceeding fourteen years.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 80
- 2006, c. 9, s. 261
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-80-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 80"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem, where bribes offered or accepted

**[[81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-81 .sectionLabel}** Every person who

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} promises, offers or gives any bribe to any officer or
  any person acting in any office or employment connected with the
  collection, management or disbursement of public money, with intent

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to influence the decision or action of that officer
    or person on any question or matter that is then pending, or may, by
    law, be brought before him in his official capacity, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to influence that officer or person to commit, or
    aid or abet in committing any fraud on the revenue, or to connive
    at, collude in, or allow or permit any opportunity for the
    commission of any such fraud, or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} accepts or receives any such bribe,

is guilty of an indictable offence and liable on conviction to a fine
not exceeding three times the amount so offered or accepted and to
imprisonment for any term not exceeding five years.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. F-10, s. 93
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Books, etc., property of Her Majesty

**[[82]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-82 .sectionLabel}** All books, papers,
accounts and documents kept or used by, or received or taken into the
possession of, any officer or person who is or has been employed in the
collection or management of the revenue or in accounting for the
revenue, by virtue of that employment, shall be deemed to be chattels
belonging to Her Majesty, and all money or valuable securities received
or taken into the possession of that officer or person by virtue of his
employment shall be deemed to be money and valuable securities belonging
to Her Majesty.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. F-10, s. 94
:::

## [PART X]{.HLabel1}[Crown Corporations]{.HTitleText1} {#h-229273 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-229274 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[83]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-83 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this Part,

  [[agent corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[agent corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a Crown
      corporation that is expressly declared by or pursuant to any other
      Act of Parliament to be an agent of the Crown; ([société
      mandataire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[appoint]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[appoint]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes elect and
      designate; ([nomination]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[appropriate Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[appropriate Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means,

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a parent Crown corporation,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the Minister specified by or pursuant to any
          other Act of Parliament as the Minister in respect of that
          corporation, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if no Minister is specified as described in
          subparagraph (i), such member of the Queen's Privy Council for
          Canada as is designated by order of the Governor in Council as
          the appropriate Minister for the corporation, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a wholly-owned subsidiary, the
        appropriate Minister, as defined in paragraph (a), for the
        parent Crown corporation that wholly owns the
        subsidiary; ([ministre de tutelle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[articles]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[articles]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} original or restated articles of incorporation,
        articles of amendment, articles of amalgamation, articles of
        continuance, articles of reorganization, articles of
        arrangement, articles of dissolution and articles of revival,
        and includes any amendments thereto, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} letters patent, memorandum of association and
        any constating documents or other documents of a like nature to
        those referred to in paragraph (a), and includes any amendments
        thereto; ([statuts]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[auditor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[auditor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a partnership of
      auditors; ([vérificateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[board of directors]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[board of directors]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a board of
      directors, by whatever name called, of a corporation and includes
      a group of persons, other than a minister of the Crown, that
      constitutes a corporation; ([conseil
      d'administration]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[by-law]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[by-law]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a by-law, by whatever name
      called, of a corporation and includes an amendment or a repeal of
      a by-law; ([règlements administratifs]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[chairperson]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[chairperson]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person occupying the
      position of chairperson, by whatever name called, of the board of
      directors of a corporation; ([président]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[charter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[charter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a corporation established by an
        Act of Parliament, the Act, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a corporation established by
        articles, the articles; ([acte constitutif]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a company or other
      body corporate wherever or however incorporated; ([personne
      morale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Crown]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Crown]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means Her Majesty in right of
      Canada; ([Sa Majesté]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Crown corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Crown corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a parent Crown
      corporation or a wholly-owned subsidiary; ([société
      d'État]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[directive]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[directive]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 1991, c. 24, s.
      21\]]{.Repealed}

  [[director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person occupying the
      position of director, by whatever name called, of a corporation
      and includes any person, other than a minister of the Crown, in a
      group of persons that constitutes a
      corporation; ([administrateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[major business or activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[major business or activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to
      a parent Crown corporation or a wholly-owned subsidiary, means a
      class of business or activity of the corporation or subsidiary
      established pursuant to subsection (10) or, if no classes are so
      established, all the business or activity of the corporation or
      subsidiary; ([activités principales]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[parent Crown corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[parent Crown corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      corporation that is wholly owned directly by the Crown, but does
      not include a departmental corporation; ([société d'État
      mère]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the regulations made
      under this Part; ([règlements]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a membership interest or
      ownership interest in a corporation; ([action]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[wholly-owned subsidiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[wholly-owned subsidiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      corporation that is wholly owned by one or more parent Crown
      corporations directly or indirectly through any number of
      subsidiaries each of which is wholly owned directly or indirectly
      by one or more parent Crown corporations. ([filiale à cent pour
      cent]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Wholly owned by Crown

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, a corporation is
  wholly owned directly by the Crown if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all of the issued and outstanding shares of the
    corporation, other than shares necessary to qualify persons as
    directors, are held, otherwise than by way of security only, by, on
    behalf of or in trust for the Crown; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all the directors of the corporation, other than
    [ex officio]{.otherLang lang="la"} directors, are appointed by the
    Governor in Council or by a minister of the Crown with the approval
    of the Governor in Council.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holding by agent corporations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2), shares held by a
  corporation that is an agent of the Crown

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} are deemed not to be held by or on behalf of the
    Crown; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} are not by reason of that fact alone shares held in
    trust for the Crown.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Wholly owned by other corporation or
  corporations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, a corporation is
  wholly owned by one or more other corporations if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all of the issued and outstanding shares of the
    corporation, other than shares necessary to qualify persons as
    directors, are held, otherwise than by way of security only, by, on
    behalf of or in trust for the one or more other corporations;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all the directors of the corporation are appointed
    by the board or boards of directors of the one or more other
    corporations; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} all the directors of the one or more other
    corporations are, by virtue of their being directors of the one or
    more other corporations, directors of the corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Affiliates

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} one corporation is an affiliate of another
    corporation if one of them is the subsidiary of the other or both
    are subsidiaries of the same corporation or each of them is
    controlled by the same person; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if two corporations are affiliates of the same
    corporation at the same time, they are deemed to be affiliates of
    each other.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsidiaries

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, a corporation is a
  subsidiary of another corporation if it is controlled by that other
  corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Control

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, a corporation with
  share capital is controlled by a person if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} shares of the corporation to which are attached
    more than fifty per cent of the votes that may be cast to elect
    directors of the corporation are held, otherwise than by way of
    security only, by, on behalf of or in trust for that person; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the votes attached to those shares are sufficient,
    if exercised, to elect a majority of the directors of the
    corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, a corporation without
  share capital is controlled by a person if that person is able to
  appoint the majority of the directors of the corporation, whether or
  not he does so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, a person is deemed to
  be appointed by another person or group of persons if that person is
  appointed on the direction of that other person or group, whether or
  not he is actually appointed by that other person or group.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Major business or activity

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} Where, in the opinion of the Governor in Council, a
  parent Crown corporation or a wholly-owned subsidiary of a parent
  Crown corporation carries on, or a parent Crown corporation and any
  wholly-owned subsidiary of the corporation carry on, more than one
  business or activity, the parent Crown corporation shall, with the
  approval of the Governor in Council, classify the businesses or
  activities for the purposes of this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 83
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (4th Supp.), s. 26
- 1991, c. 24, s. 21
- 2005, c. 30, s. 133(E)
:::

Version](section-83-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 83"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-229336 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Binding on the Crown

**[[84]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-84 .sectionLabel}** For greater certainty,
this Part is binding on the Crown.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1984, c. 31, s. 11
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption for Bank of Canada

- **[[85]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Divisions I to IV, except for
  sections 89.8 to 89.92, 131.1 and 154.01, do not apply to the Bank of
  Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption for Canada Pension Plan
  Investment Board

  [(1.01)]{.lawlabel} Divisions I to IV, except for section 154.01, do
  not apply to the Canada Pension Plan Investment Board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exempted Crown corporations

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Divisions I to IV, except for sections 89.8 to
  89.92, subsection 105(2) and sections 113.1, 119, 131 to 148 and
  154.01, do not apply to the Canada Council for the Arts, the Canadian
  Broadcasting Corporation, the International Development Research
  Centre or the National Arts Centre Corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption for Telefilm Canada

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} Divisions I to IV, except for sections 89.8 to
  89.92, subsection 105(2) and sections 113.1, 119, 131 to 148 and
  154.01 and subject to subsection 21(2) of the [Telefilm Canada
  Act](/eng/acts/C-16), do not apply to Telefilm Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption for RCMP and security
  services

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Divisions I to IV do not apply to any Crown
  corporation incorporated or acquired, with the written authorization
  of the appropriate Minister,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} by or on behalf of the Royal Canadian Mounted
    Police for the purpose of performing its functions under the laws of
    Canada;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} by or on behalf of the Communications Security
    Establishment for the purpose of performing its functions under the
    [Communications Security Establishment Act](/eng/acts/C-35.3); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by or on behalf of any service established by an
    Act of Parliament to collect information and intelligence respecting
    the security of Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption for federal member
  institution

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Divisions I to IV do not apply to a member
  institution, as defined in section 2 of the [Canada Deposit Insurance
  Corporation Act](/eng/acts/C-3), any shares of which are held by the
  Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation as the result of the granting of
  an exemption referred to in section 10.01 of that Act.

- [(3) and (4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2016, c. 7, s. 163\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 85
- R.S., 1985, c. 46 (1st Supp.), s. 7

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to wholly-owned subsidiaries

- **[[86]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-86 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Each
  parent Crown corporation shall take such steps in relation to the
  articles, by-laws and management of each wholly-owned subsidiary of
  the corporation, if any, as are necessary to ensure that the
  businesses, activities and other affairs of the subsidiary are carried
  on in accordance with this Part and the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may declare any provision of
  this Part that applies only to parent Crown corporations to apply to a
  wholly-owned subsidiary, and the provision applies, with such
  modifications as the circumstances require, to that wholly-owned
  subsidiary as if it were a parent Crown corporation.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 86
- 1991, c. 24, s. 22
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inconsistencies

**[[87]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-87 .sectionLabel}** Except as otherwise
expressly provided, in the event of any inconsistency between the
provisions of this Part and the provisions of any other Act of
Parliament, the provisions of this Part prevail.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1984, c. 31, s. 11
:::

### [DIVISION I]{.HLabel2}[Corporate Affairs]{.HTitleText2} {#h-229370 .Subheading}

#### [Accountability to Parliament]{.HTitleText3} {#h-229371 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Accountability to Parliament

**[[88]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88 .sectionLabel}** Each Crown corporation is
ultimately accountable, through the appropriate Minister, to Parliament
for the conduct of its affairs.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1984, c. 31, s. 11
:::

#### [Directives]{.HTitleText3} {#h-229376 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Directives by Governor in Council

- **[[89]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-89 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of the appropriate
  Minister, give a directive to any parent Crown corporation, if the
  Governor in Council is of the opinion that it is in the public
  interest to do so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before a directive is given to a parent Crown
  corporation, the appropriate Minister shall consult the board of
  directors of the corporation with respect to the content and effect of
  the directive.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1991, c. 24, s. 23\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tabling in Parliament

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The appropriate Minister shall cause a copy of any
  directive given to a parent Crown corporation to be laid before each
  House of Parliament on any of the first fifteen days on which that
  House is sitting after the directive is given.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1991, c. 24, s. 23\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of implementation

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Forthwith after implementing a directive and
  completing any actions required to be taken in connection therewith, a
  parent Crown corporation shall notify the appropriate Minister that
  the directive has been implemented.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} No directive shall be given to the Standards Council
  of Canada with respect to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the manner in which voluntary standardization is
    promoted; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the provision of financial assistance to or for the
    benefit of a particular person or group of persons.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 89
- 1991, c. 24, s. 23
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Implementation

- **[[89.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-89.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The directors of a parent Crown
  corporation to which a directive is given shall ensure that the
  directive is implemented in a prompt and efficient manner and, if in
  so doing they act in accordance with section 115, they are not
  accountable for any consequences arising from the implementation of
  the directive.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Best interests

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Compliance by a parent Crown corporation with a
  directive is deemed to be in the best interests of the corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[directive]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[directive]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}
  means

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a directive given pursuant to subsection 89(1),
    94(2) or 114(3);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a direction given under subsection 5(2) of the
    [Canada Mortgage and Housing Corporation Act](/eng/acts/C-7),
    subsection 9(2) of the [Canadian Commercial Corporation
    Act](/eng/acts/C-14) or subsection 11(1) of the [Canadian Dairy
    Commission Act](/eng/acts/C-15); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a directive given under subsection 22(1) of the
    [Canada Post Corporation Act](/eng/acts/C-10).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 24, s. 24
- 1998, c. 10, s. 173
- 2014, c. 20, s. 188
- 2015, c. 3, ss. 94(F), 175(F)
:::

[Previous
Version](section-89.1-20140619.html "Link to previous version of section 89.1"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Implementation of World Trade Organization Agreement]{.HTitleText3} {#h-229406 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Directive

- **[[89.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-89.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsections 85(1) to
  (1.2), the Governor in Council may give a directive pursuant to
  subsection 89(1) to any parent Crown corporation for the purpose of
  implementing any provision of the WTO Agreement that pertains to that
  Crown corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of
  the Treasury Board and the appropriate Minister made at the request of
  a Crown corporation, make such regulations in relation to that
  corporation as the Governor in Council considers necessary for the
  purpose of implementing any provision of the WTO Agreement that
  pertains to that corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In subsections (1) and (2), [[WTO
  Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} has the meaning given to the word
  [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} by subsection 2(1) of the [World
  Trade Organization Agreement Implementation Act](/eng/acts/W-11.8).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1994, c. 47, s. 116
- 2006, c. 9, s. 263
:::

Version](section-89.2-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 89.2"){.PITLink}
