:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acceptance and release of security

- **[[156]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-156 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  appropriate Minister responsible for the recovery or collection of a
  debt or obligation due or payable to Her Majesty or a claim by Her
  Majesty may accept any security in respect of the debt, obligation or
  claim and may realize on the security, assign or sell Her Majesty's
  interest in it or discharge, release or otherwise dispose of the
  security or Her Majesty's interest in it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Partial disposition

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The power to dispose of any security or Her Majesty's
  interest in any security pursuant to this section may be exercised
  with respect to any part of the security or interest.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Treasury Board may make regulations respecting
  the acceptance and disposition of security or Her Majesty's interest
  in security under subsection (1), including regulations prescribing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} what may be accepted as security; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} terms and conditions on which security may be
    accepted or on which security or Her Majesty's interest in security
    may be realized on or disposed of.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 156
- 1991, c. 24, s. 46
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information already in Public Accounts or
estimates

**[[157]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-157 .sectionLabel}** Where it appears to the
Governor in Council that any account, statement, return or document
required by any Act of Parliament or otherwise to be laid before one or
both Houses of Parliament contains the same information as or less
information than is contained in the Public Accounts or in any estimates
of expenditures submitted to Parliament, the Governor in Council may
direct that the account, statement, return or other document be
discontinued, and thereafter it need not be prepared or laid before
either House of Parliament.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 157
- 1991, c. 24, s. 47
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of Treasury Board records

**[[158]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158 .sectionLabel}** A document purporting
to be a copy of an entry in the records of the Treasury Board certified
by the Secretary, a Deputy Secretary, or an Assistant Secretary of the
Treasury Board or by the Comptroller General of Canada, a Deputy
Comptroller General of Canada or by an Assistant Comptroller General of
Canada is, without proof of the signature or of the official character
of the person purporting to have signed it, admissible in any court of
justice and has the same probative force as the original document would
have if it were proven in the ordinary way.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. F-10, s. 97
- 1980-81-82-83, c. 170, s. 22
- 1984, c. 31, s. 12
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[other financial
institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[159]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-159 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[other financial institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  means

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a member of the Canadian Payments Association, a
    local cooperative credit society, a fiscal agent or a financial
    institution with which the Receiver General establishes accounts for
    the deposit of public money pursuant to subsection 17(2); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other financial institution that accepts or
    receives an instruction for payment issued pursuant to section 35.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No charge for certain cheques, etc.

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No bank or other financial institution shall make a
  charge

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for cashing a cheque or other instrument drawn on
    the Receiver General or on the Receiver General's account in the
    Bank of Canada or any other bank or other financial institution;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for honouring or otherwise giving value for any
    other instruction for payment issued pursuant to section 35; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of any cheque or other instruction for
    payment drawn in favour of the Receiver General, the Government of
    Canada, any department or any public officer in the officer's
    official capacity, and tendered for deposit to the credit of the
    Receiver General.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deposits of the Government of Canada,
  etc.

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in subsection (2) shall be construed as
  prohibiting any arrangement between the Government of Canada and a
  bank or other financial institution concerning compensation for
  services performed by the bank or institution for the Government of
  Canada or interest to be paid on any or all deposits of the Government
  of Canada with the bank or institution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure of information

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Any bank or other financial institution of a class
  prescribed by regulations that accepts for deposit an instrument or
  instruction for payment described in subsection (2) must disclose any
  information prescribed by regulations made under subsection 160(2) in
  any statement of account or other transaction record that is provided
  to the holder of the account receiving the deposit.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 159
- 1991, c. 24, s. 48
- [2024, c. 17, s. 180](#1467594-1483760){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1467594-1483760.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-159-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 159"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[160]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-160 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may make regulations for carrying the purposes and
  provisions of this Act into effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Classes of financial institutions and
  disclosure of information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  prescribing classes of financial institutions and any information
  disclosure requirements referred to in subsection 159(4).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. F-11, s. 160
- [2024, c. 17, s. 181](#1467596-1483767){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1467596-1483767.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-160-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 160"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Management or protection of computer
systems

- **[[161]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-161 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  appropriate Minister, any public servant employed in a department, any
  employee of a Crown corporation or any person acting on behalf of a
  department or Crown corporation who performs duties relating to the
  management or protection of computer systems of the department or the
  Crown corporation may take reasonable measures for such purposes,
  including the interception of private communications in circumstances
  specified in paragraph 184(2)(e) of the [Criminal
  Code](/eng/acts/C-46).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Privacy protection

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), with respect to an
  interception referred to in subsection (1), the appropriate Minister
  shall take reasonable measures to ensure that only data that is
  essential to identify, isolate or prevent harm to the computer system
  will be used or retained.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in this section affects any other lawful
  authority to intercept, use, retain, access or disclose a private
  communication.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[computer
  system]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, [[computer
  system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device that, or a group of
  interconnected or related devices one or more of which,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} contains computer programs or other data; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} pursuant to computer programs,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} performs logic and control, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} may perform any other function.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2004, c. 12, s. 20
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meaning of [[listed tax
law]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[162]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-162 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[listed tax law]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) and the
    [Income Tax Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._945);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the [Income Tax Conventions Interpretation
    Act](/eng/acts/I-4);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) and any
    regulations made under that Act;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) and any
    regulations made under that Act;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the Air Travelers Security Charge Act and any
    regulations made under that Act;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14) and any
    regulations made under that Act; or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) and any
    regulations made under that Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tabling of list  --- legislative
  proposals

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall table in the House of Commons, on
  or before the fifth day on which the House of Commons is sitting after
  October 31 of a particular fiscal year, a list of the specific
  legislative proposals to amend listed tax laws

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that the Government publicly announced before April
    1 of the fiscal year preceding the particular fiscal year; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that have not been enacted or made before the date
    of tabling in substantially the same form as the proposal or in a
    form that reflects consultations and deliberations relating to the
    proposal.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The list referred to in subsection (2) shall not
  include a specific legislative proposal that has been publicly
  withdrawn by the Government or an announcement of a general intention
  to develop a specific legislative proposal.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The obligation to table does not apply in respect of
  a particular fiscal year if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} there are no specific legislative proposals to be
    included in the list referred to in subsection (2); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the fifth day on which the House of Commons is
    sitting after October 31 of the particular fiscal year is less than
    12 months after the last general election.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 20, s. 31
:::

## RELATED PROVISIONS {#related-provisions .scheduleTitleText}

- --- 2003, c. 22, s. 67

  - ###### Deemed designated portions {#deemed-designated-portions .MarginalNote}

    [67]{.lawlabel} Every portion of the public service of Canada
    designated by the Governor in Council before the day on which
    section 8 of this Act comes into force to be part of the public
    service for the purposes of sections 11, 12 and 13 of the [Financial
    Administration Act](/eng/acts/F-11) is deemed to be a portion of the
    federal public administration designated by the Governor in Council
    for the purpose of paragraph (d) of the definition [[public
    service]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 11(1) of the
    [Financial Administration Act](/eng/acts/F-11), as enacted by
    section 8 of this Act.

- --- 2005, c. 15, s. 6

  - ###### President of the Public Service Human Resources Management Agency of Canada {#president-of-the-public-service-human-resources-management-agency-of-canada .MarginalNote}

    [6]{.lawlabel} The person occupying the position of President of the
    Public Service Human Resources Management Agency of Canada on the
    day on which section 1 comes into force or the day on which section
    4 or 5 operates according to its terms, as the case may be, is
    deemed, as of that day, to be appointed to that position under
    subsection 6(2.1) of the [Financial Administration
    Act](/eng/acts/F-11) and continues to occupy it until another person
    is appointed to that position under that subsection.

- --- 2005, c. 26, s. 18(1), as amended by 2005, c. 26, par. 27(2)(a)(E)

  - ###### Definitions {#definitions .MarginalNote}

    - [18]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply
      in this section.

      [[former agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

      :   [[former agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the portion of the
          federal public administration known as the Economic
          Development Agency of Canada for the Regions of
          Quebec. ([ancienne agence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

      [[new agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

      :   [[new agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Economic
          Development Agency of Canada for the Regions of Quebec
          established by section 8. ([nouvelle agence]{.DefinedTermLink
          lang="fr"})

- --- 2005, c. 26, par. 18(7)(b)

  - ###### References {#references .MarginalNote}

    - [18]{.lawlabel} [(7)]{.lawlabel} A reference to the former agency
      in any of the following is deemed to be a reference to the new
      agency:

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Schedule I.1 to the [Financial Administration
        Act](/eng/acts/F-11);

- --- 2006, c. 5, s. 16

  - ###### Definitions {#definitions-1 .MarginalNote}

    [16]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in sections 17 to
    19.

    [[former agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[former agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the portion of the
        federal public administration known as the Public Health Agency
        of Canada. ([ancienne agence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

    [[new agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[new agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Public Health
        Agency of Canada established under section 3. ([nouvelle
        agence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- --- 2006, c. 5, s. 19

  - ###### References {#references-1 .MarginalNote}

    - [19]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} A reference to the former agency
      in any of the following schedules and orders is deemed to be a
      reference to the new agency:

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Schedules I.1 and IV to the [Financial
        Administration Act](/eng/acts/F-11);

    - ###### Deputy head {#deputy-head .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} The designation of a person as deputy head of the
      former agency in any order of the Governor in Council made
      pursuant to paragraph 29(e) of the [Canadian Security Intelligence
      Service Act](/eng/acts/C-23) or to the definition [[deputy
      head]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 2(1) of the [Public
      Service Employment Act](/eng/acts/P-33.01) is deemed to be a
      designation of the Chief Public Health Officer as deputy head of
      the new agency.

- --- 2011, c. 24, s. 161, as amended by 2013, c. 33, s. 233, 2019, c.
  29, par. 375(1)(i) and 2024, c. 15, s. 338

  - ###### Maximum payment {#maximum-payment .MarginalNote}

    - [161]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} There may be paid out of the
      Consolidated Revenue Fund for each fiscal year beginning on or
      after April 1, 2014, on the requisition of the Minister of
      Infrastructure and Communities or of the Minister of Indigenous
      Services, in accordance with terms and conditions approved by the
      Treasury Board, a sum of not more than the amount determined in
      accordance with subsection (2) to provinces, territories,
      municipalities, municipal associations, provincial, territorial
      and municipal entities and First Nations for the purpose of
      municipal, regional and First Nations infrastructure.

    - ###### Calculation of sum {#calculation-of-sum .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the fiscal year beginning on April 1, 2014,
      the sum that may be paid under subsection (1) is \$2,000,000,000.
      For each subsequent fiscal year, the sum may be \$100,000,000 more
      than the sum that may be paid for the previous fiscal year, if the
      amount determined in accordance with the formula set out in
      subsection (3) exceeds by \$100,000,000 or more the sum that may
      be paid for the previous fiscal year.

    - ###### Formula {#formula .MarginalNote}

      [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2), the formula
      is as follows:

      ::: Subsection
      A x 1.02^B^

      where

      [A]{.dfn}
      :   is \$2,000,000,000; and

      [B]{.dfn}
      :   is the number obtained by subtracting 2013 from the number of
          the year in which the fiscal year in question begins.
      :::

- --- 2012, c. 19, s. 209

  - ###### Definitions {#definitions-2 .MarginalNote}

    [209]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Division, [[appropriate
    Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, [[Crown
    corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} and
    [[department]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} have the same meanings as in
    section 2 of the [Financial Administration Act](/eng/acts/F-11).

- --- 2012, c. 31, s. 162

  - ###### Setting of fees --- [Canada Shipping Act, 2001](/eng/acts/C-10.15) {#setting-of-fees-canada-shipping-act-2001 .MarginalNote}

    - [162]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} A classification society that
      provides, during the period beginning on July 1, 2007 and ending
      on the day before the day on which this Act receives royal assent,
      any of the following services in the exercise of powers or the
      performance of duties under the [Canada Shipping Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/C-10.15) in accordance with an agreement or
      arrangement entered into by the Minister of Transport under
      paragraph 10(1)(c) of that Act may set the fees to be paid to it
      for those services:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} services related to a Canadian maritime
        document;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} services related to any approvals or
        certifications; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the conduct or witnessing of tests.

    - ###### Not public money {#not-public-money .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} The fees referred to in subsection (1) are not
      public money within the meaning of the [Financial Administration
      Act](/eng/acts/F-11) and the [User Fees Act](/eng/acts/U-3.7) does
      not apply in respect of them.

    - ###### Non-application of certain regulations {#non-application-of-certain-regulations .MarginalNote}

      [(3)]{.lawlabel} The regulations made under paragraph 35(1)(g) of
      the [Canada Shipping Act, 2001](/eng/acts/C-10.15) do not apply in
      respect of any service referred to in any of paragraphs (1)(a)
      to (c) that is provided, during the period beginning on July 1,
      2007 and ending on the day before the day on which this Act
      receives royal assent, by a classification society in the exercise
      of powers or the performance of duties under that Act in
      accordance with an agreement or arrangement entered into by the
      Minister of Transport under paragraph 10(1)(c) of that Act.

- --- 2012, c. 31, s. 163

  - ###### Setting of fees --- [Canada Shipping Act](/eng/acts/S-9) {#setting-of-fees-canada-shipping-act .MarginalNote}

    - [163]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} A classification society that
      provides, during the period beginning on January 1, 1999 and
      ending on June 30, 2007, any of the following services in the
      exercise of powers or the performance of duties under the [Canada
      Shipping Act](/eng/acts/S-9) in accordance with an agreement or
      arrangement entered into by the Minister of Transport under
      paragraph 8(1)(c) of that Act may set the fees to be paid to it
      for those services:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} services related to an inspection certificate;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} services related to any approvals or
        certifications; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the conduct or witnessing of tests.

    - ###### Not public money {#not-public-money-1 .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} The fees referred to in subsection (1) are not
      public money within the meaning of the [Financial Administration
      Act](/eng/acts/F-11) and are not subject to subsection 408(2) of
      the [Canada Shipping Act](/eng/acts/S-9).

    - ###### [User Fees Act](/eng/acts/U-3.7) {#user-fees-act .MarginalNote}

      [(3)]{.lawlabel} The [User Fees Act](/eng/acts/U-3.7) does not
      apply in respect of the fees referred to in subsection (1).

- --- 2019, c. 13, par. 82(1)(b)

  - ###### References {#references-2 .MarginalNote}

    - [82]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} A reference to the former
      department in any of the following is deemed to be a reference to
      the new department:

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Schedules I.1, V and VI to the [Financial
        Administration Act](/eng/acts/F-11);

- --- 2024, c. 17, s. 263

  - ###### Non-application of provisions {#non-application-of-provisions .MarginalNote}

    - [263]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Section 91 and subsection 100(1)
      of the [Financial Administration Act](/eng/acts/F-11) do not apply
      to or in respect of the subsidiary referred to in section 261 or
      any of its wholly owned subsidiaries.

    - ###### Application of provisions {#application-of-provisions .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsections 89(1), (4) and (6) and section 89.1
      of the [Financial Administration Act](/eng/acts/F-11) apply to or
      in respect of the subsidiary as if it were a parent Crown
      corporation.
